TL;DR: SLIDERS是一个通过结构化推理（将文档信息提取到关系数据库并用SQL进行推理）来应对长文档集合问答挑战的框架，通过数据协调阶段处理重复、不一致和不完整的记录，在多个基准测试上超越了GPT-4.1等强基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实场景的文档问答需要跨多个文档及文档不同部分综合证据，但固定LLM上下文窗口无法容纳不断增长的文档集合。常见的分块策略会产生聚合瓶颈——随着分块数量增加，系统仍需组合和推理大量提取出的证据。

Method: SLIDERS框架包含三个关键组件：(1) 将文档中的关键信息提取到关系数据库中；(2) 通过数据协调阶段（利用来源信息、提取理由和元数据）检测并修复重复、不一致和不完整的记录，确保局部提取的表示在全局层面保持一致；(3) 使用SQL而非拼接文本进行可扩展的结构化推理。

Result: 在三个现有长上下文基准测试上，SLIDERS超越了所有基线模型，平均超过GPT-4.1 6.6分；在两个新基准测试上（分别包含390万和3600万token），SLIDERS分别比次优基线模型高出约19分和32分。

Conclusion: SLIDERS通过结构化数据表示和SQL推理有效解决了长文档集合的问答难题，即使所有基线测试集都适配在强基础LLM的上下文窗口内，SLIDERS仍能显著超越它们，证明了该框架在处理超长文档时的优势。

Abstract: Real-world document question answering is challenging. Analysts must synthesize evidence across multiple documents and different parts of each document. However, any fixed LLM context window can be exceeded as document collections grow. A common workaround is to decompose documents into chunks and assemble answers from chunk-level outputs, but this introduces an aggregation bottleneck: as the number of chunks grows, systems must still combine and reason over an increasingly large body of extracted evidence. We present SLIDERS, a framework for question answering over long document collections through structured reasoning. SLIDERS extracts salient information into a relational database, enabling scalable reasoning over persistent structured state via SQL rather than concatenated text. To make this locally extracted representation globally coherent, SLIDERS introduces a data reconciliation stage that leverages provenance, extraction rationales, and metadata to detect and repair duplicated, inconsistent, and incomplete records. SLIDERS outperforms all baselines on three existing long-context benchmarks, despite all of them fitting within the context window of strong base LLMs, exceeding GPT-4.1 by 6.6 points on average. It also improves over the next best baseline by ~19 and ~32 points on two new benchmarks at 3.9M and 36M tokens, respectively.

</details>


### [77] [CLARITY: A Framework and Benchmark for Conversational Language Ambiguity and Unanswerability in Interactive NL2SQL Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22313)
*Tabinda Sarwar,Farhad Moghimifar,Cong Duy Vu Hoang,Xiaoxiao Ma,Shawn Chang Xu,Fahimeh Saleh,Poorya Zaremoodi,Avirup Sil,Katrin Kirchhoff*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Clarity框架，用于生成包含多面歧义和多样化用户行为的NL2SQL基准测试。实验表明，现有NL2SQL系统（包括基于强LLM的系统）在多面歧义下性能显著下降，虽能检测歧义但难以准确定位和解决底层模式级来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业界NL2SQL系统经常遇到模糊或不可回答的查询，尤其在用户澄清不完整的交互场景中。现有基准测试通常假设单一歧义来源并依赖用户交互来解决，忽略了现实中的失败模式。

Method: 提出Clarity框架，采用约束驱动的流水线，将可执行的SQL转换为包含多面歧义的模糊查询，并增强基于上下文的对话延续和模式级元数据。在Spider和BIRD数据集上进行评估。

Result: 领先的NL2SQL系统（包括基于强LLM的系统）在多面歧义下性能显著下降。系统通常能检测到歧义，但在准确定位和解决底层模式级歧义来源方面存在困难。

Conclusion: 工业级NL2SQL系统需要更鲁棒的歧义检测和解决机制，以应对多源、多轮的复杂歧义场景。

Abstract: NL2SQL systems deployed in industry settings often encounter ambiguous or unanswerable queries, particularly in interactive scenarios with incomplete user clarification. Existing benchmarks typically assume a single source of ambiguity and rely on user interaction for resolution, overlooking realistic failure modes.
  We introduce Clarity, a framework for automatically generating an NL2SQL benchmark with multi-faceted ambiguities and diverse user behaviors across both single- and multi-turn settings. Using a constraint-driven pipeline, Clarity transforms executable SQL into ambiguous queries, augmented with grounded conversational continuations and schema-level metadata.
  Empirical evaluation on Spider and BIRD shows that leading NL2SQL systems, including those based on strong LLMs, suffer significant performance degradation under multi-faceted ambiguity. While these systems often detect ambiguity, they struggle to accurately localize and resolve the underlying schema-level sources. Our results highlight the need for more robust ambiguity detection and resolution in industry-grade NL2SQL systems.

</details>


### [78] [Context-Fidelity Boosting: Enhancing Faithful Generation through Watermark-Inspired Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22335)
*Weixu Zhang,Fanghua Ye,Qiang Gao,Jian Li,Haolun Wu,Yuxing Tian,Sijing Duan,Nan Du,Xiaolong Li,Xue Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Context-Fidelity Boosting (CFB)，一种轻量级的解码时框架，通过对源支持令牌施加对数概率调整来减少LLM的事实忠实性幻觉，无需重训练或架构变更，在摘要和问答任务中有效提升忠实度指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型常常生成与输入上下文矛盾或忽略上下文信息的忠实性幻觉内容，现有方法需要重训练或架构修改，缺乏轻量级且通用的解决方案。

Method: 受水印技术中的对数概率整形启发，CFB基于令牌从输入上下文获得的支持程度，在解码时施加加性令牌级对数概率调整。提出三种策略：静态增强（固定偏置）、上下文感知增强（基于有无上下文的下一个令牌分布差异缩放偏置）、令牌感知增强（根据源位置注意力和源范围语义相似度估计的局部相关性进一步重新分配自适应偏置）。

Result: 在多个开源LLM上的摘要和问答任务实验中，CFB一致性地提升了忠实性指标，且生成开销极小。

Conclusion: CFB是一种轻量、通用、无需重训练或架构变更的解码时框架，能有效减少LLM的事实忠实性幻觉，且与多种LLM兼容。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often produce content that contradicts or overlooks information provided in the input context, a phenomenon known as faithfulness hallucination. In this paper, we propose Context-Fidelity Boosting (CFB), a lightweight and general decoding-time framework that reduces such hallucinations by increasing the generation probability of source-supported tokens. Motivated by logit-shaping principles from watermarking techniques, CFB applies additive token-level logit adjustments based on a token's degree of support from the input context. Specifically, we develop three boosting strategies: static boosting, which applies a fixed bias to source-supported tokens; context-aware boosting, which scales this bias using the divergence between next-token distributions with and without context; and token-aware boosting, which further redistributes the adaptive bias according to local relevance estimated from source-position attention and source-scoped semantic similarity. CFB requires no retraining or architectural changes, making it compatible with a wide range of LLMs. Experiments on summarization and question answering tasks across multiple open-source LLMs show that CFB consistently improves faithfulness metrics with minimal generation overhead. Our implementation is fully open-sourced.

</details>


### [79] [Preference Heads in Large Language Models: A Mechanistic Framework for Interpretable Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22345)
*Weixu Zhang,Ye Yuan,Changjiang Han,Yuxing Tian,Zipeng Sun,Linfeng Du,Jikun Kang,Hong Kang,Xue Liu,Haolun Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DPS（差分偏好引导）框架，通过因果掩码分析识别LLM中编码用户偏好的“偏好注意力头”，并在推理时对比有无偏好头的预测差异来实现可控个性化。实验表明该方法在保持内容连贯性的同时提升了个性化保真度，计算开销低，且提供了个性化机制的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM个性化方法将模型视为黑箱，依赖提示工程或用户数据微调，缺乏对个性化内部机制的理解和可控性。

Method: (1) 通过因果掩码分析识别偏好注意力头；(2) 计算每个注意力头的偏好贡献分数(PCS)；(3) 解码时对比有无偏好头的预测，放大个性化与通用logits的差异以增强偏好对齐的续写。

Result: 在多个LLM和个人化基准上持续提升个性化保真度，同时保持内容连贯性并带来低计算开销。

Conclusion: DPS不仅实现有效的可控个性化，还从机制上揭示了个性化在Transformer架构中产生的位置和方式，提供了可解释性视角。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) exhibit strong implicit personalization ability, yet most existing approaches treat this behavior as a black box, relying on prompt engineering or fine tuning on user data. In this work, we adopt a mechanistic interpretability perspective and hypothesize the existence of a sparse set of Preference Heads, attention heads that encode user specific stylistic and topical preferences and exert a causal influence on generation. We introduce Differential Preference Steering (DPS), a training free framework that (1) identifies Preference Heads through causal masking analysis and (2) leverages them for controllable and interpretable personalization at inference time. DPS computes a Preference Contribution Score (PCS) for each attention head, directly measuring its causal impact on user aligned outputs. During decoding, we contrast model predictions with and without Preference Heads, amplifying the difference between personalized and generic logits to selectively strengthen preference aligned continuations. Experiments on widely used personalization benchmarks across multiple LLMs demonstrate consistent gains in personalization fidelity while preserving content coherence and low computational overhead. Beyond empirical improvements, DPS provides a mechanistic explanation of where and how personalization emerges within transformer architectures. Our implementation is publicly available.

</details>


### [80] [CNSL-bench: Benchmarking the Sign Language Understanding Capabilities of MLLMs on Chinese National Sign Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22367)
*Rui Zhao,Xuewen Zhong,Xiaoyun Zheng,Jinsong Su,Yidong Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个针对多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的中国国家通用手语基准CNSL-bench，涵盖官方标准化的词典内容、多模态对齐数据及多种手语构形形式。实验发现，当前MLLMs在手语理解上显著落后于人类表现，且存在跨模态和构形系统的系统性差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在手语理解方面的能力（尤其是在多模态场景下）尚未得到充分探索，缺乏系统性的评估基准。

Method: 构建CNSL-bench基准，其特点包括：1) 以官方《国家通用手语词典》为权威依据；2) 提供文本描述、插图和手语视频的多模态对齐数据；3) 覆盖空中书写、手指拼写和汉语手指字母表等多种手部构形形式。基于该基准评估了21个开源和商业MLLMs。

Result: 当前MLLMs在手语理解任务上显著低于人类水平；存在跨输入模态和手语构形形式的系统性性能差异；推理能力的提升无法完全解决性能局限；不同模型的指令遵循鲁棒性差异显著。

Conclusion: 尽管多模态建模取得进展，现有MLLMs在手语理解上仍有明显不足，需要进一步研究以缩小与人类表现的差距。

Abstract: Sign language research has achieved significant progress due to the advances in large language models (LLMs). However, the intrinsic ability of LLMs to understand sign language, especially in multimodal contexts, remains underexplored. To address this limitation, we introduce CNSL-bench, the first comprehensive Chinese em{National Sign Language benchmark designed for evaluating multimodal large language models (MLLMs) in sign language understanding. The proposed CNSL-bench is characterized by: 1) Authoritative grounding, as it is anchored to the officially standardized \textit{National Common Sign Language Dictionary, mitigating ambiguity from regional or non-canonical variants and ensuring consistent semantic definitions; 2) Multimodal coverage, providing aligned textual descriptions, illustrative images, and sign language videos; and 3) Articulatory diversity, supporting fine-grained analysis across key manual articulatory forms, including air-writing, finger-spelling, and the Chinese manual-alphabet. Using CNSL-bench, we extensively evaluate 21 open-source and proprietary up-to-date MLLMs. Our results reveal that, despite recent advances in multimodal modeling, current MLLMs remain substantially inferior to human performance, exhibiting systematic disparities across input modalities and manual articulatory forms. Additional diagnostic analyses suggest that several performance limitations persist beyond improvements in reasoning and that instruction-following robustness varies substantially across models.

</details>


### [81] [Selective Contrastive Learning For Gloss Free Sign Language Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22374)
*Changhao Lai,Rui Zhao,Xuewen Zhong,Jinsong Su,Yidong Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SCL-SLT方法，通过选择性对比学习和负样本选择策略，解决手语翻译中随机批次对比学习存在的噪声和无效负样本问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SLT系统采用CLIP-like视觉-语言预训练进行跨模态对齐，但随机批次对比学习中的负样本存在两个问题：(1) 批次依赖导致负样本数量有限；(2) 语义相似甚至相同的配对被错误标记为负样本，引入噪声和矛盾的对齐监督信号。

Method: (1) 通过轨迹分析追踪训练中负样本视频-文本相似度变化，发现仅少数负样本被持续推远，多数呈现异质且非递减的相似度动态；(2) 提出SCL-SLT，包含Pair Selection (PS)策略：利用参考检查点的相似度动态对候选负样本评分，并通过课程学习逐步强调更具挑战性的负样本，构建更有效的对比监督。

Result: 轨迹分析表明随机批次负样本中仅少数具有有效对齐作用，多数为无信息负样本；所提方法能增强对比监督并减少噪声/语义无效负样本的影响。

Conclusion: 通过选择性对比学习有效缓解了无标注手语翻译中负样本噪声问题，提升了跨模态对齐质量。

Abstract: Sign language translation (SLT) converts continuous sign videos into spoken-language text, yet it remains challenging due to the intrinsic modality mismatch between visual signs and written text, particularly in gloss-free settings. Recent SLT systems increasingly adopt CLIP-like Vision-Language pretraining (VLP) for cross-modal alignment, but the random in-batch contrast provides few, batch-dependent negatives and may mislabel semantically similar (or even identical) pairs as negatives, introducing noisy and potentially inconsistent alignment supervision. In this work, we first conduct a preliminary trajectory-based analysis that tracks negative video-text similarity over training. The results show that only a small subset of negatives exhibits the desired behavior of being consistently pushed away, while the remaining negatives display heterogeneous and often non-decreasing similarity dynamics, suggesting that random in-batch negatives are frequently uninformative for effective alignment. Inspired by this, we propose Selective Contrastive Learning for SLT (SCL-SLT) with a Pair Selection (PS) strategy. PS scores candidate negatives using similarity dynamics from reference checkpoints and constructs mini-batches via a curriculum that progressively emphasizes more challenging negatives, thereby strengthening contrastive supervision while reducing the influence of noisy or semantically invalid negatives.

</details>


### [82] [Measuring and Mitigating Persona Distortions from AI Writing Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22503)
*Paul Röttger,Kobi Hackenburg,Hannah Rose Kirk,Christopher Summerfield*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模实验发现，AI写作辅助会导致作者人设扭曲——读者感知到的作者观点、个性、情感和人口统计学特征均发生系统性偏差（如显得更偏执、更积极、更倾向特权群体），作者虽反感这种扭曲却仍偏好AI辅助文本；通过奖励模型训练可缓解部分扭曲，但会降低用户接受度，表明AI辅助的期望特性与不良特性之间存在难以解耦的纠缠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估AI写作辅助如何扭曲作者人设（即读者对作者信念、个性和身份的感知），揭示这种扭曲在现实条件下的普遍性和持续性，及其对公共话语、信任和民主协商的潜在影响。

Method: 三阶段大规模实验：2,939名作者在有/无AI辅助下撰写政治观点段落，11,091名读者盲评段落，涵盖29个社会感知维度（政治观点、写作质量、个性、情感、人口统计学等）；随后基于实验数据（10,008段文本，2,903,596条评分）训练奖励模型，用以引导AI输出更忠实于作者立场。

Result: AI写作辅助在所有维度上产生人设扭曲：作者显得更偏执、更有能力、更积极正面，人口统计学特征向特权群体偏移；作者反对多数扭曲但继续偏好AI辅助文本；模型层面缓解扭曲后用户接受度下降。

Conclusion: AI写作辅助导致的人设扭曲普遍且持续存在，即使在人工监督的现实条件下也难以消除；期望特性（如提升写作质量）与不良特性（如人设扭曲）之间存在难以解耦的纠缠，这对AI广泛采用背景下的公共话语和民主协商具有重要意义。

Abstract: Hundreds of millions of people use artificial intelligence (AI) for writing assistance. Here, we evaluated how AI writing assistance distorts writer personas - their perceived beliefs, personality, and identity. In three large-scale experiments, writers (N=2,939) wrote political opinion paragraphs with and without AI assistance. Separate groups of readers (N=11,091) blindly evaluated these paragraphs across 29 socially salient dimensions of reader perception, spanning political opinion, writing quality, writer personality, emotions, and demographics. AI writing assistance produced persona distortions across all dimensions: with AI, writers seemed more opinionated, competent, and positive, and their perceived demographic profile shifted towards more privileged groups. Writers objected to many of the observed distortions, yet continued to prefer AI-assisted text even when made aware of them. We successfully mitigated objectionable persona distortions at the model level by training reward models on our experimental data (10,008 paragraphs, 2,903,596 ratings) to steer AI outputs towards faithful representation of writer stance. However, this came at a cost to user acceptance, suggesting an entanglement between desirable and undesirable properties of AI writing assistance that may be difficult to resolve. Together, our findings demonstrate that persona distortions from AI writing assistance are pervasive and persistent even under realistic conditions of human oversight, which carries implications for public discourse, trust, and democratic deliberation that scale with AI adoption.

</details>


### [83] [Aggregate vs. Personalized Judges in Business Idea Evaluation: Evidence from Expert Disagreement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22517)
*Wataru Hirota,Tomoki Taniguchi,Tomoko Ohkuma,Kosuke Takahashi,Takahiro Omi,Kosuke Arima,Takuto Asakura,Chung-Chi Chen,Tatsuya Ishigaki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了在商业创意评估中，由于专家之间存在显著分歧，自动评判器应逼近聚合共识还是建模个体评估者。通过引入PBIG-DATA数据集（约3000个来自领域专家的评分），比较了三种评判配置（仅模板零样本、聚合评判器、个性化评判器），发现个性化评判器与对应评估者的一致性更高，表明聚合标签在多元化评估场景中可能不可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业创意评估依赖多维度标准（如可行性、新颖性、差异化、用户需求、市场规模等），专家判断常存在分歧。现有NLP基准难以直接迁移，因此需要研究在这种分歧情境下，自动评判器应如何设计——是逼近聚合共识还是建模个体评估者。

Method: 构建PBIG-DATA数据集（约3000个评分，300个基于专利的产品创意，6个商业维度），分析专家分歧的结构化特征；对比三种评判器：仅模板零样本评判器、基于混合评估者历史的聚合评判器、基于目标评估者评分历史的个性化评判器。

Result: 专家在细粒度序数评分上存在显著分歧，但在粗粒度选择上一致性较高，表明分歧是结构化异质性而非随机噪声。个性化评判器在所有维度和模型规模下与对应评估者对齐程度均高于聚合评判器；且仅在个性化条件下，评估者一致性才与评判器生成推理的相似度相关。

Conclusion: 聚合标签在多元化评估场景中可能是脆弱的目标，研究结果支持在商业创意评估中采用以评估者条件化（个性化）的评判器设计。

Abstract: Evaluating LLM-generated business ideas is often harder to scale than generating them. Unlike standard NLP benchmarks, business idea evaluation relies on multi-dimensional criteria such as feasibility, novelty, differentiation, user need, and market size, and expert judgments often disagree. This paper studies a methodological question raised by such disagreement: should an automatic judge approximate an aggregate consensus, or model evaluators individually? We introduce PBIG-DATA, a dataset of approximately 3,000 individual scores across 300 patent-grounded product ideas, provided by domain experts on six business-oriented dimensions: specificity, technical validity, innovativeness, competitive advantage, need validity, and market size. Analyses show substantial expert disagreement on fine-grained ordinal scores, while agreement is higher under coarse selection, suggesting structured heterogeneity rather than random noise. We then compare three judge configurations: a rubric-only zero-shot judge, an aggregate judge conditioned on mixed evaluator histories, and a personalized judge conditioned on the target evaluator's scoring history. Across dimensions and model sizes, personalized judges align more closely with the corresponding evaluator than aggregate judges, and evaluator agreement correlates with similarity of judge-generated reasoning only under personalized conditioning. These results indicate that pooled labels can be a fragile target in pluralistic evaluation settings and motivate evaluator-conditioned judge designs for business idea assessment.

</details>


### [84] [RouteLMT: Learned Sample Routing for Hybrid LLM Translation Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22520)
*Yingfeng Luo,Hongyu Liu,Dingyang Lin,Kaiyan Chang,Chenglong Wang,Bei Li,Quan Du,Tong Xiao,Jingbo Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RouteLMT，一种基于边际增益预测的高效路由方法，用于LLM机器翻译中的混合系统，在不依赖外部模型的情况下实现更优的质量-预算帕累托边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在机器翻译中表现优异但部署成本过高，混合系统通过小模型处理大部分请求、大模型选择性路由部分请求来平衡成本与质量，但现有路由策略依赖启发式方法、外部预测器或绝对质量估计，未能捕捉大模型相对小模型的实际改进程度。

Method: 将路由建模为预算分配问题，以边际增益（大模型相对于小模型的改进）作为最优信号；提出RouteLMT，通过探测小翻译模型的提示词表示来预测期望边际增益，无需外部模型或假设解码。

Result: RouteLMT在质量-预算帕累托边界上优于启发式方法、质量/难度估计基线；引入简单防护变体可缓解严重质量损失。

Conclusion: 基于边际增益的路由策略能更有效地分配预算，RouteLMT为LLM机器翻译的混合系统提供了高效、实用的路由方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable performance in Machine Translation (MT), but deploying them at scale remains prohibitively expensive. A widely adopted remedy is the hybrid system paradigm, which balances cost and quality by serving most requests with a small model and selectively routing a fraction to a large model. However, existing routing strategies often rely on heuristics, external predictors, or absolute quality estimation, which fail to capture whether the large model actually provides a worthwhile improvement over the small one. In this paper, we formulate routing as a budget allocation problem and identify marginal gain, i.e., the large model's improvement over the small model, as the optimal signal for budgeted decisions. Building on this, we propose \textbf{RouteLMT} (routing for LLM-based MT), an efficient in-model router that predicts this expected gain by probing the small translators prompt-token representation, without requiring external models or hypothesis decoding. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our RouteLMT outperforms heuristics, quality/difficulty estimation baselines, achieving a superior quality-budget Pareto frontier. Furthermore, we analyze regression risks and show that a simple guarded variant can mitigate severe quality losses.

</details>


### [85] [Controllable Spoken Dialogue Generation: An LLM-Driven Grading System for K-12 Non-Native English Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22542)
*Haidong Yuan,Haokun Zhao,Wanshi Xu,Songjun Cao,Qingyu Zhou,Long Ma,Hongjie Fan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于CSE（中国英语能力等级量表）的、面向K-12非母语英语学习者的LLM输出适配框架，核心贡献是DDPO（多样性驱动策略优化）算法，该算法通过多轮GRPO在保持对话多样性的同时提升质量，实现了低超出词率和高多样性，并增强了对话的自然性与教学价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在非母语K-12英语教学场景中，常因输出语言水平与学习者实际能力不匹配而无法满足教学需求，存在显著的"能力错配"问题。

Method: 提出了一个面向熟练度对齐的框架，基于CSE四层分级系统控制词汇复杂度；核心贡献是DDPO（多样性驱动策略优化）算法，一种基于多轮GRPO的方法，在保持对话多样性的同时整体优化对话质量。

Result: DDPO方法显著优于传统方法，实现了低超出词率（低OOV率）和高多样性，同时提升了对话的自然性和教学价值。

Conclusion: 该框架虽然基于CSE构建，但具有良好的灵活性，可适配其他教育标准；所有模型、数据和代码将开源，为K-12非沉浸式环境下的个性化英语口语练习提供可扩展的平台。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often fail to meet the pedagogical needs of K-12 English learners in non-native contexts due to a proficiency mismatch. To address this widespread challenge, we introduce a proficiency-aligned framework that adapts LLM outputs to learner abilities, using China's national curriculum (CSE) as a representative case. Our framework enables precise control over lexical complexity through a four-tier grading system, supported by a comprehensive suite of new resources: graded vocabulary lists and a multi-turn dialogue corpus.
  Our core technical contribution is the \textbf{DDPO} algorithm,Diversity Driven Policy Optimization, a multi-turn GRPO-based approach designed to preserve dialogue diversity while holistically optimizing dialogue quality. This method significantly outperforms conventional approaches, achieving low out-of-vocabulary rates and high diversity while enhancing conversational naturalness and pedagogical value. While grounded in the CSE, our framework is designed for flexibility and can be readily adapted to other educational standards. Our models, data, and code will all be open-sourced, providing a scalable platform for personalized English speaking practice that effectively addresses the unique challenges faced by K-12 learners in non-immersive environments.

</details>


### [86] [Learning Evidence Highlighting for Frozen LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22565)
*Shaoang Li,Yanhang Shi,Yufei Li,Mingfu Liang,Xiaohan Wei,Yunchen Pu,Fei Tian,Chonglin Sun,Frank Shyu,Luke Simon,Sandeep Pandey,Xi Liu,Jian Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HiLight是一种证据强调框架，通过轻量级Actor在长文本中插入高亮标记来突出关键证据，使冻结的LLM解算器在不改变输入的情况下提升推理性能，无需证据标签并可零样本迁移到不同规模的解算器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在长文本和嘈杂上下文中常遗漏决定性证据，现有方法（压缩或重写输入）可能丢失或扭曲证据信息。

Method: 训练轻量级Emphasis Actor在原始上下文中关键片段周围插入最小高亮标记；将高亮视为弱监督决策问题，使用强化学习仅凭解算器任务奖励优化Actor，无需证据标签或修改/访问解算器。

Result: 在序列推荐和长上下文问答任务中，HiLight持续优于强提示基线和自动提示优化基线；所学策略可零样本迁移到更小/更大及API-based的未见解算器。

Conclusion: HiLight通过分离证据选择与推理，以非破坏性强调方式有效提升LLM长上下文推理能力，且Actor捕获了可复用的通用证据结构而非过拟合单个模型。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) can reason well, yet often miss decisive evidence when it is buried in long, noisy contexts. We introduce HiLight, an Evidence Emphasis framework that decouples evidence selection from reasoning for frozen LLM solvers. HiLight avoids compressing or rewriting the input, which can discard or distort evidence, by training a lightweight Emphasis Actor to insert minimal highlight tags around pivotal spans in the unaltered context. A frozen Solver then performs downstream reasoning on the emphasized input. We cast highlighting as a weakly supervised decision-making problem and optimize the Actor with reinforcement learning using only the Solver's task reward, requiring no evidence labels and no access to or modification of the Solver. Across sequential recommendation and long-context question answering, HiLight consistently improves performance over strong prompt-based and automated prompt-optimization baselines. The learned emphasis policy transfers zero-shot to both smaller and larger unseen Solver families, including an API-based Solver, suggesting that the Actor captures genuine, reusable evidence structure rather than overfitting to a single backbone.

</details>


### [87] [Dharma, Data and Deception: An LLM-Powered Rhetorical Analysis of Cow-Urine Health Claims on YouTube](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22606)
*Sheza Munir,Ratna Kandala,Anamta Khan,Deepti,Joyojeet Pal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究分析了100个YouTube视频转录文本（关于牛尿作为保健疗法的宣传与辟谣），利用多种大语言模型（GPT-4系列、GPT-5、Gemini 2.5 Pro、Mistral Medium 3）基于14类说服策略分类法进行标注，发现宣传者主要依赖功效诉求和社会证明，而辟谣者强调权威和反驳。人工评估取得90.1%的标注一致性，验证了分类法的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 健康错误信息在社交媒体上持续构成严峻挑战，尤其当文化传统与看似科学的说法交织时。这些动态既是全球性的，也是深度本土化的，需要细致分析特定文化背景下的争议。作者旨在通过牛尿保健这一具体文化案例，探究错误信息的传播策略。

Method: 收集100个YouTube视频转录文本（涵盖宣传与辟谣牛尿保健的两类内容）；采用14类说服策略分类法；使用GPT-4、GPT-4o、GPT-4.1、GPT-5、Gemini 2.5 Pro、Mistral Medium 3等多种LLM进行自动标注；通过人工评估子集（90.1%一致性）验证分类可靠性。

Result: 宣传者主要使用功效诉求（efficacy appeals）和社会证明（social proof）策略；辟谣者则侧重权威（authority）和反驳（rebuttal）策略。人类评估显示90.1%的标注者间一致性，证明分类法和验证流程可靠。

Conclusion: 该工作推进了错误信息分析的计算方法，展示了LLM如何支持大规模文化话语在线研究，为理解文化特定语境下的健康错误信息传播提供了方法论基础。

Abstract: Health misinformation remains one of the most pressing challenges on social media, particularly when cultural traditions intersect with scientific-sounding claims. These dynamics are not only global but also deeply local, manifesting in culturally specific controversies that require careful analysis. Motivated by this, we examine 100 YouTube transcripts that promote or debunk cow urine (gomutra) as a health remedy, focusing on rhetorical strategies such as appeals to authority, efficacy appeals, and conspiracy framing. We employ large language models (LLMs) including GPT-4, GPT-4o, GPT-4.1, GPT-5, Gemini 2.5 Pro, and Mistral Medium 3 to annotate transcripts using a 14-category taxonomy of persuasive tactics. Our analysis reveals that promoters predominantly rely on efficacy appeals and social proof, while debunkers emphasize authority and rebuttal. Human evaluation of a subset of annotations yielded 90.1\% inter-annotator agreement, confirming the reliability of our taxonomy and validation process. This work advances computational methods for misinformation analysis and demonstrates how LLMs can support large-scale studies of cultural discourse online.

</details>


### [88] [From graphemic dependence to lexical structure: a Markovian perspective on Dante's Commedia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22626)
*Angelo Maria Sabatini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过元音-辅音(V/C)编码对但丁《神曲》进行符号化表示，构建四状态马尔可夫链模型，发现从《地狱篇》到《天堂篇》的书写记忆指数呈递增趋势，并通过三字母组分析与特定词汇环境建立联系，揭示了局部依赖、词汇分布、拼写编码与文本宏观组织之间的结构化交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何通过简单的符号化文本表示（元音-辅音编码）和概率模型来捕捉诗歌文本中从局部依赖到全局组织的多层级结构特征。

Method: 采用元音-辅音(V/C)编码将文本转换为符号序列，构建四状态马尔可夫链模型，定义书写记忆指数衡量持久性与交替模式的平衡；通过三字母组分析识别关键配置（"书写探针"），并结合分类分析识别各诗篇特有词汇。

Result: 从《地狱篇》到《天堂篇》的书写记忆指数呈轻微但持续递增；趋势由一组受限的循环配置驱动；含两次转移的配置更常跨越词边界，转移较少的配置集中于词内结构；部分信号受拼写现象（尤其省音形式）影响；分类分析识别出诗篇特有词汇。

Conclusion: 简单概率模型应用于符号文本表示，能够揭示局部依赖、词汇分布、拼写编码与文本宏观组织之间的结构化交互，为连接局部符号动态与更高层次文本组织提供了可解释的分析框架。

Abstract: This study investigates the structural organisation of Dante's Divina Commedia through a symbolic representation based on vowel-consonant (V/C) encoding. Modelling the resulting sequence as a four-state Markov chain yields a parsimonious index of graphemic memory, capturing the balance between persistence and alternation patterns.
  Across the poem, this index exhibits a slight but consistent increase from the Inferno to the Paradiso, indicating a directional shift in local dependency structure. Trigram-level analysis shows that this trend is driven by a restricted set of recurrent configurations, interpreted as graphemic probes linking the Markov representation to identifiable lexical environments in the text.
  These probes display distinct behaviours: configurations involving two transitions more frequently emerge across word boundaries, reflecting interactions between adjacent tokens, whereas configurations with fewer transitions are largely confined to intra-lexical structures. Part of the signal is further shaped by orthographic phenomena, particularly apostrophised forms, highlighting the role of writing conventions alongside phonological and lexical organisation.
  A complementary classification analysis identifies cantica-specific terms, providing lexical anchors through which graphemic probes can be related to the structure of the poem. This organisation is reflected not only in the separation of the three cantiche, but also in a continuous trajectory across the text.
  Overall, the results show that simple probabilistic models applied to symbolic text representations can uncover structured interactions between local dependencies, lexical distribution, orthographic encoding, and large-scale organisation, providing an interpretable framework for linking local symbolic dynamics to higher-level textual organisation.

</details>


### [89] [Identifying and typifying demographic unfairness in phoneme-level embeddings of self-supervised speech recognition models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22631)
*Felix Herron,Solange Rossato,Alexandre Allauzen,François Portet*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出框架区分ASR系统中音素嵌入的两种错误类型：随机误差（高方差）和系统误差（嵌入偏置）。研究发现两种错误都存在于音素嵌入中，随机误差对公平性的阻碍更大，且公平性增强算法无法改变这两种错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代ASR系统对不同说话者群体表现不一致，现有研究缺乏对语音编码模型错误类型的细致理解，特别是高表现和低表现群体之间嵌入结构的差异。

Method: 提出区分音素建模中随机误差（高方差）和系统误差（嵌入偏置）的框架；通过在单一弱势群体上训练音素分类探针来检测群体级别的嵌入偏置；分析说话者及群体的音素方差与预测准确率的关系。

Result: 仅在弱势群体上训练音素分类探针有时能提升该群体性能（证明存在群体级嵌入偏置）；音素方差高的说话者和群体恰好是音素预测准确率低的群体；公平性增强算法（领域增强和对抗训练）无法改变探针训练收益和随机嵌入误差水平。

Conclusion: ASR系统中音素嵌入同时存在随机误差和系统误差，两者都是导致群体级不公平的候选原因，但随机误差对公平性的阻碍更大；现有公平性增强算法未能有效解决这些问题。

Abstract: Modern automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems have been observed to function better for certain speaker groups (SGs) than others, despite recent gains in overall performance. One potential impediment to progress towards fairer ASR is a more nuanced understanding of the types of modeling errors that speech encoder models make, and in particular the difference between the structure of embeddings for high-performance and low-performance SGs. This paper proposes a framework typifying two types of error that can occur in modeling phonemes in ASR systems: random error/high variance in phoneme embedding, vs systematic error/embedding bias. We find that training phoneme classification probes only on a single, typically disadvantaged SG, sometimes improves performance for that SG, which is evidence for the existence of SG-level bias in phoneme embeddings. On the other hand, we find that speakers and SGs with higher levels of phoneme variance are the same as those with worse phoneme prediction accuracy. We conclude that both types of error are present in phoneme embeddings and both are candidate causes for SG-level unfairness in ASR, though random error is likely a greater hindrance to fairness than systematic error. Furthermore, we find that finetuning encoder models using a fairness-enhancing algorithm (domain enhancing and adversarial training) changes neither the benefits of in-domain phoneme classification probe training, nor measured levels of random embedding error.

</details>


### [90] [BERAG: Bayesian Ensemble Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Knowledge-based Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22678)
*Jinghong Chen,Jingbiao Mei,Guangyu Yang,Bill Byrne*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出贝叶斯集成检索增强生成（BERAG）及其微调方法（BEFT），通过将语言模型分别置于每个检索文档上而非拼接上下文，利用贝叶斯规则逐token更新文档后验概率作为集成权重，从而缓解"迷失在中间"效应、降低计算成本并实现清晰的归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAG将文档拼接为单一上下文存在三个问题：1）难以归因单个文档的贡献；2）"迷失在中间"效应导致长上下文中相关文档被忽略；3）拼接方式计算成本随上下文长度二次增长（尤其视觉数据场景）。限制上下文长度虽可缓解但会降低检索召回带来的性能收益。

Method: 提出BERAG框架，将语言模型独立应用于每个检索文档，将文档后验概率视为集成权重，在生成过程中逐token使用贝叶斯规则更新这些权重。该方法实现了概率重排序、并行内存使用和文档贡献的清晰归因。同时提出BEFT微调方法以适配该框架。

Result: 在基于知识的视觉问答任务（Document VQA、多模态"大海捞针"基准）上相比标准RAG有显著提升，有效缓解了"迷失在中间"效应。文档后验概率可用于检测证据不足并触发拒答，文档剪枝可实现比标准RAG更快的解码速度。

Conclusion: BERAG/BEFT为RAG提供了一种替代拼接范式的新框架，兼顾了归因性、计算效率和长上下文推理能力，尤其适用于需要处理大量检索文档的视觉问答场景。

Abstract: A common approach to question answering with retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is to concatenate documents into a single context and pass it to a language model to generate an answer. While simple, this strategy can obscure the contribution of individual documents, making attribution difficult and contributing to the ``lost-in-the-middle'' effect, where relevant information in long contexts is overlooked. Concatenation also scales poorly: computational cost grows quadratically with context length, a problem that becomes especially severe when the context includes visual data, as in visual question answering. Attempts to mitigate these issues by limiting context length can further restrict performance by preventing models from benefiting from the improved recall offered by deeper retrieval. We propose Bayesian Ensemble Retrieval-Augmented Generation (BERAG), along with Bayesian Ensemble Fine-Tuning (BEFT), as a RAG framework in which language models are conditioned on individual retrieved documents rather than a single combined context. BERAG treats document posterior probabilities as ensemble weights and updates them token by token using Bayes' rule during generation. This approach enables probabilistic re-ranking, parallel memory usage, and clear attribution of document contribution, making it well-suited for large document collections. We evaluate BERAG and BEFT primarily on knowledge-based visual question answering tasks, where models must reason over long, imperfect retrieval lists. The results show substantial improvements over standard RAG, including strong gains on Document Visual Question Answering and multimodal needle-in-a-haystack benchmarks. We also demonstrate that BERAG mitigates the ``lost-in-the-middle'' effect. The document posterior can be used to detect insufficient grounding and trigger deflection, while document pruning enables faster decoding than standard RAG.

</details>


### [91] [CRAFT: Clustered Regression for Adaptive Filtering of Training data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22693)
*Parthasarathi Panda,Asheswari Swain,Subhrakanta Panda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CRAFT提出了一种基于聚类的两阶段训练数据筛选方法，通过源分布匹配和条件目标分布优化，在英印翻译任务上以极快速度（26.86秒）实现了43.34 BLEU，显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语料库规模增长至数千万级别，全量微调变得昂贵且不必要，需要一种高效的方法从大规模数据中选择高质量子集进行微调。

Method: CRAFT采用两阶段选择：第一阶段通过k-means聚类的比例预算分配匹配验证集源分布；第二阶段在每个源聚类内，选择目标嵌入条件期望距离最小的训练对。方法不依赖特定向量化方式，并提供了KL散度的理论保证。

Result: 在英印翻译任务上，从3300万NLLB句对中选择数据微调mBART（LoRA），CRAFT达到43.34 BLEU，比TSDS（41.21）高2.13分，且选择速度快40倍以上；整个流水线（TF-IDF向量化）在CPU上一分钟内完成；选择耗时26.86秒，比TAROT（75.6秒）快2.8倍。

Conclusion: CRAFT在保证选择质量的同时大幅提升了速度，是一种高效、可扩展的训练数据筛选方法，适用于大规模序列到序列模型的微调数据选择。

Abstract: Selecting a small, high-quality subset from a large corpus for fine-tuning is increasingly important as corpora grow to tens of millions of datapoints, making full fine-tuning expensive and often unnecessary. We propose CRAFT (Clustered Regression for Adaptive Filtering of Training data), a vectorization-agnostic selection method for training sequence-to-sequence models. CRAFT decomposes the joint source-target distribution and performs a two-stage selection: (i) match the validation source distribution through proportional budget allocation across k-means clusters, and (ii) within each source cluster, select training pairs whose target embeddings minimize a conditional expected distance derived from the validation target distribution. We prove that proportional cluster allocation bounds the continuous KL divergence between selected and validation distributions, with the residual controlled by cluster diameters. We evaluate CRAFT on English-Hindi translation by selecting training data from 33 million NLLB sentence pairs and fine-tuning mBART via LoRA. CRAFT achieves 43.34 BLEU, outperforming TSDS (41.21) by 2.13 points on the same candidate pool and encoder while completing selection over 40 times faster. With TF-IDF vectorization, the entire pipeline completes in under one minute on CPU. TAROT achieves 45.61 BLEU, but CRAFT completes selection in 26.86 seconds versus TAROT's 75.6 seconds, a 2.8 time speedup.

</details>


### [92] [Thinking Without Words: Efficient Latent Reasoning with Abstract Chain-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22709)
*Keshav Ramji,Tahira Naseem,Ramón Fernandez Astudillo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Abstract Chain-of-Thought，一种离散潜在推理后训练机制，通过保留词汇表中的短序列标记替代自然语言CoT，在显著减少推理token的同时保持可比性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长链式思维推理虽有效但推理成本高，非语言推理方法性能不足，亟需一种兼顾效率与性能的推理机制。

Method: 采用策略迭代式预热循环，交替进行（1）通过掩码从语言CoT瓶颈化并执行SFT，（2）通过带码本的约束解码进行自蒸馏；之后在约束解码下用热启动强化学习优化抽象序列生成。

Result: 推理token减少高达11.6倍，在数学推理、指令跟随和多跳推理中表现相当，跨语言模型家族泛化良好，抽象词汇呈现幂律分布。

Conclusion: 后训练潜在推理机制通过学习抽象推理语言可实现高效推理，为将推理过程压缩为离散潜在序列提供了可行路径。

Abstract: While long, explicit chains-of-thought (CoT) have proven effective on complex reasoning tasks, they are costly to generate during inference. Non-verbal reasoning methods have emerged with shorter generation lengths by leveraging continuous representations, yet their performance lags behind verbalized CoT. We propose $\textbf{Abstract Chain-of-Thought}$, a discrete latent reasoning post-training mechanism in which the language model produces a short sequence of tokens from a reserved vocabulary in lieu of a natural language CoT, before generating a response. To make previously unseen ''abstract'' tokens useful, we introduce a policy iteration-style warm-up loop that alternates between (i.) bottlenecking from a verbal CoT via masking and performing supervised fine-tuning, and (ii.) self-distillation by training the model to generate abstract tokens from the prompt alone via constrained decoding with the codebook. After warm-up, we optimize the generation of abstract sequences with warm-started reinforcement learning under constrained decoding. Abstract-CoT achieves up to $11.6\times$ fewer reasoning tokens while demonstrating comparable performance across mathematical reasoning, instruction-following, and multi-hop reasoning, and generalizes across language model families. We also find an emergent power law distribution over the abstract vocabulary, akin to those seen in natural language, that evolves across the training phases. Our findings highlight the potential for post-training latent reasoning mechanisms that enable efficient inference through a learned abstract reasoning language.

</details>


### [93] [Representational Harms in LLM-Generated Narratives Against Global Majority Nationalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22749)
*Ilana Nguyen,Harini Suresh,Thema Monroe-White,Evan Shieh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了主流大语言模型在开放式叙事生成中，按国籍身份呈现的描绘性伤害（有害刻板印象、抹除和单维刻画）。发现全球南方身份在权力中性故事中被低估，而在从属角色中被过度代表（超过50倍），且美国国籍线索会放大伤害程度，该现象无法用谄媚效应解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLMs在高风险场景（如庇护申请面试）中的应用日益广泛，但现有研究对LLMs如何描绘不同国籍身份的系统性偏见评估不足，特别是针对全球多数群体的文化危害。

Method: 对主流LLMs输入开放式叙事生成提示，系统性地改变提示中的国籍线索（美国/非美国），分析输出文本中不同国籍身份的呈现频率、角色类型（权力中性/从属/主导）及刻板印象内容。

Result: 1) 少数族裔国籍在权力中性故事中代表性不足，在从属角色中被过度代表（超过50倍）；2) 美国国籍线索会放大危害程度；3) 即使将美国国籍替换为非美国国籍，危害仍然存在，表明无法用谄媚效应解释。

Conclusion: LLMs存在系统性的国籍描绘性伤害，呼吁开发以全球多数群体视角为中心的方法论，批判性地审视美国中心LLMs在全球范围内的分类、监控和错误表征问题。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for text generation tasks from everyday use to high-stakes enterprise and government applications, including simulated interviews with asylum seekers. While many works highlight the new potential applications of LLMs, there are risks of LLMs encoding and perpetuating harmful biases about non-dominant communities across the globe. To better evaluate and mitigate such harms, more research examining how LLMs portray diverse individuals is needed. In this work, we study how national origin identities are portrayed by widely-adopted LLMs in response to open-ended narrative generation prompts. Our findings demonstrate the presence of persistent representational harms by national origin, including harmful stereotypes, erasure, and one-dimensional portrayals of Global Majority identities. Minoritized national identities are simultaneously underrepresented in power-neutral stories and overrepresented in subordinated character portrayals, which are over fifty times more likely to appear than dominant portrayals. The degree of harm is amplified when US nationality cues (e.g., ``American'') are present in input prompts. Notably, we find that the harms we identify cannot be explained away via sycophancy, as US-centric biases persist even when replacing US nationality cues with non-US national identities in the prompts. Based on our findings, we call for further exploration of cultural harms in LLMs through methodologies that center Global Majority perspectives and challenge the uncritical adoption of US-based LLMs for the classification, surveillance, and misrepresentation of the majority of our planet.

</details>


### [94] [How Do AI Agents Spend Your Money? Analyzing and Predicting Token Consumption in Agentic Coding Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22750)
*Longju Bai,Zhemin Huang,Xingyao Wang,Jiao Sun,Rada Mihalcea,Erik Brynjolfsson,Alex Pentland,Jiaxin Pei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了AI智能体在编程任务中的token消耗模式，分析了8个前沿LLM在SWE-bench Verified上的轨迹。发现：智能体任务token消耗极大（是代码推理/聊天的1000倍以上），输入token是主要成本；同一任务token消耗差异可达30倍，但更高消耗不带来更高准确率；不同模型token效率差异巨大（Kimi-K2和Claude-Sonnet-4.5平均比GPT-5多消耗150万+token）；人类感知的任务难度与实际token成本弱对齐；前沿模型无法准确预测自身token消耗（相关性最高仅0.39），且系统性地低估实际成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI智能体在复杂人类工作流中被广泛采用，LLM token消耗快速增长。当智能体部署在需要大量token的任务上时，自然产生三个问题：(1) AI智能体把token花在哪里？(2) 哪些模型更token高效？(3) 智能体能否在任务执行前预测其token用量？本文旨在首次系统回答这些问题。

Method: 对8个前沿LLM在SWE-bench Verified上的轨迹进行分析，评估模型在任务执行前预测自身token成本的能力。通过比较不同模型、不同任务间的token消耗模式进行系统研究。

Result: (1) 智能体任务token消耗是代码推理/聊天的1000倍以上，输入token驱动主要成本；(2) 同一任务token消耗差异可达30倍，更高消耗不提升准确率，准确率在中等成本达到峰值；(3) Kimi-K2和Claude-Sonnet-4.5平均比GPT-5多消耗150万+token；(4) 人类专家评定的任务难度与实际token成本弱对齐；(5) 模型无法准确预测自身token用量，相关性最高仅0.39，且系统性地低估实际成本。

Conclusion: 本研究为AI智能体的经济学提供了新见解，揭示了智能体token消耗的高度随机性、模型间巨大效率差异以及人类感知与实际计算成本之间的根本差距，可激励未来研究方向。

Abstract: The wide adoption of AI agents in complex human workflows is driving rapid growth in LLM token consumption. When agents are deployed on tasks that require a significant amount of tokens, three questions naturally arise: (1) Where do AI agents spend the tokens? (2) Which models are more token-efficient? and (3) Can agents predict their token usage before task execution? In this paper, we present the first systematic study of token consumption patterns in agentic coding tasks. We analyze trajectories from eight frontier LLMs on SWE-bench Verified and evaluate models' ability to predict their own token costs before task execution. We find that: (1) agentic tasks are uniquely expensive, consuming 1000x more tokens than code reasoning and code chat, with input tokens rather than output tokens driving the overall cost; (2) token usage is highly variable and inherently stochastic: runs on the same task can differ by up to 30x in total tokens, and higher token usage does not translate into higher accuracy; instead, accuracy often peaks at intermediate cost and saturates at higher costs; (3) models vary substantially in token efficiency: on the same tasks, Kimi-K2 and Claude-Sonnet-4.5, on average, consume over 1.5 million more tokens than GPT-5; (4) task difficulty rated by human experts only weakly aligns with actual token costs, revealing a fundamental gap between human-perceived complexity and the computational effort agents actually expend; and (5) frontier models fail to accurately predict their own token usage (with weak-to-moderate correlations, up to 0.39) and systematically underestimate real token costs. Our study offers new insights into the economics of AI agents and can inspire future research in this direction.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [95] [ResRank: Unifying Retrieval and Listwise Reranking via End-to-End Joint Training with Residual Passage Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22180)
*Xiaojie Ke,Shuai Zhang,Liansheng Sun,Yongjin Wang,Hengjun Jiang,Xiangkun Liu,Cunxin Gu,Jian Xu,Guanjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ResRank提出统一检索-重排序框架，通过Encoder-LLM将每个候选段落压缩为单个嵌入，配合残差连接和余弦相似度评分机制，在保持或超越现有方法排序效果的同时实现零生成token、每段落仅处理一个token的高效推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的listwise重排序虽效果领先，但存在两个关键瓶颈：一是"lost in the middle"现象导致长输入时排序质量下降，二是推理延迟随序列长度超线性增长，难以工业部署。

Method: 受多模态LLM启发，采用Encoder-LLM将每个候选段落压缩为单个embedding，输入至Reranker-LLM进行listwise排序；引入残差连接结构缓解压缩表示空间与排序空间之间的不对齐；用一步余弦相似度评分替代传统自回归解码；设计双阶段、多任务、端到端联合优化策略。

Result: 在TREC Deep Learning和八个BEIR基准数据集上，ResRank在排序效果上与现有方法相当或更优，同时实现零生成token、每段落仅处理一个token，显著提升了效果与效率的平衡。

Conclusion: ResRank通过段落压缩、残差连接和一步评分机制，从根本上解决了LLM重排序中长输入质量下降和推理延迟高的双重挑战，为工业部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) based listwise reranking has emerged as the dominant paradigm for achieving state-of-the-art ranking effectiveness in information retrieval. However, its reliance on feeding full passage texts into the LLM introduces two critical bottlenecks: the "lost in the middle" phenomenon degrades ranking quality as input length grows, and the inference latency scales super-linearly with sequence length, rendering it impractical for industrial deployment. In this paper, we present ResRank, a unified retrieval-reranking framework that fundamentally addresses both challenges. Inspired by multimodal LLMs that project visual inputs into compact token representations, ResRank employs an Encoder-LLM to compress each candidate passage into a single embedding, which is then fed alongside the query text into a Reranker-LLM for listwise ranking. To alleviate the misalignment between the compressed representation space and the ranking space, we introduce a residual connection structure that combines encoder embeddings with contextualized hidden states from the reranker. Furthermore, we replace the conventional autoregressive decoding with a one-step cosine-similarity-based scoring mechanism, eliminating the generation bottleneck entirely. ResRank is trained through a carefully designed dual-stage, multi-task, end-to-end joint optimization strategy that simultaneously trains the encoder and reranker, achieving learning objective alignment between retrieval and reranking while substantially reducing training complexity. Extensive experiments on TREC Deep Learning and eight BEIR benchmark datasets demonstrate that ResRank achieves competitive or superior ranking effectiveness compared to existing approaches while requiring zero generated tokens and processing only one token per passage, yielding a fundamentally better balance between effectiveness and efficiency.

</details>


### [96] [Rethinking Semantic Collaborative Integration: Why Alignment Is Not Enough](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22195)
*Maolin Wang,Dongze Wu,Jianing Zhou,Hongyu Chen,Beining Bao,Yu Jiang,Chenbin Zhang,Chang Wang,Jian Liu,Lei Sha*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文质疑了LLM增强推荐系统中将语义表示与协同表示进行全局对齐的主流范式，提出两者应视为部分共享、本质异构的视图。通过互补性感知诊断发现低对齐度与高互补性，主张从对齐中心转向互补融合中心的设计范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM增强推荐系统默认假设语义表示与协同表示编码共享潜在实体，且更强的对齐能带来更好的推荐效果。本文形式化了"全局低复杂度对齐假设"，认为该假设过强且与现实推荐场景的结构不匹配，可能导致局部结构扭曲、视图特有信号丢失和信息多样性降低。

Method: 提出互补性感知诊断框架，包括：重叠度量、唯一命中贡献分析和理论融合上限计算。在稀疏推荐基准上进行实证分析，并结合受控对齐探针实验（低容量映射），检验对齐假设的有效性。

Result: 实证发现：(1) 语义视图与协同视图在项目层面一致性低；(2) 存在显著的理论融合增益，表明两者高度互补；(3) 低容量映射仅能捕捉共享成分，无法恢复完整协同几何结构，尤其在分布偏移下表现更差。

Conclusion: 对齐不应被当作默认的融合原则，推荐系统应从对齐中心建模转向互补融合中心的设计范式，选择性整合共享因子同时保留私有信号。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have become an important semantic infrastructure for modern recommender systems. A prevailing paradigm integrates LLM-derived semantic embeddings with collaborative representations via representation alignment, implicitly assuming that the two views encode a shared latent entity and that stronger alignment yields better results. We formalize this assumption as the global low-complexity alignment hypothesis and argue that it is stronger than necessary and often structurally mismatched with real-world recommendation settings. We propose a complementary perspective in which semantic and collaborative representations are treated as partially shared yet fundamentally heterogeneous views, each containing both shared and view-specific factors. Under this shared-plus-private latent structure, enforcing global geometric alignment may distort local structure, suppress view-specific signals, and reduce informational diversity. To support this perspective, we develop complementarity-aware diagnostics that quantify overlap, unique-hit contribution, and theoretical fusion upper bounds. Empirical analyses on sparse recommendation benchmarks reveal low item-level agreement between semantic and collaborative views and substantial oracle fusion gains, indicating strong complementarity. Furthermore, controlled alignment probes show that low-capacity mappings capture only shared components and fail to recover full collaborative geometry, especially under distribution shift. These findings suggest that alignment should not be treated as the default integration principle. We advocate a shift from alignment-centric modeling to complementarity fusion-centric, complementarity-aware design, where shared factors are selectively integrated while private signals are preserved. This reframing provides a principled foundation for the next generation of LLM-enhanced recommender systems.

</details>


### [97] [Objective Shaping with Hard Negatives: Windowed Partial AUC Optimization for RL-based LLM Recommenders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22504)
*Wentao Shi,Qifan Wang,Chen Chen,Fei Liu,Dongfang Liu,Xu Liu,Wanli Ma,Junfeng Pan,Linhong Zhu,Fuli Feng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了在LLM推荐系统中，使用GRPO进行强化学习优化时，随机负采样等价于最大化AUC，而与Top-K推荐存在错配；使用波束搜索负采样可将目标重构成部分AUC，从而更好对齐Top-K指标。基于此，提出WPAUC及其高效优化方法TAWin，在四个数据集上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经验上波束搜索负样本优于随机负样本，但其背后的机制尚不清晰，本文旨在从优化目标角度解释这一现象，并借此设计更优的训练方法。

Method: 理论分析GRPO在二元奖励下的优化目标等价于最大化AUC；波束搜索负采样将目标重塑为部分AUC；提出WPAUC（窗口化部分AUC）和TAWin（阈值自适应窗口重加权）方法以实现高效优化。

Result: 在四个真实数据集上验证了理论分析，并取得一致的SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 波束搜索负样本通过将优化目标从AUC转向部分AUC来改善与Top-K指标的匹配；提出的WPAUC和TAWin方法能更直接地对齐Top-K推荐性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) effectively optimizes Large Language Model (LLM)-based recommenders by contrasting positive and negative items. Empirically, training with beam-search negatives consistently outperforms random negatives, yet the mechanism is not well understood. We address this gap by analyzing the induced optimization objective and show that: (i) Under binary reward feedback, optimizing LLM recommenders with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is theoretically equivalent to maximizing the Area Under the ROC Curve (AUC), which is often misaligned with Top-$K$ recommendation; and (ii) Replacing random negatives with beam-search negatives reshapes the objective toward partial AUC, improving alignment with Top-$K$ metrics. Motivated by this perspective, we introduce Windowed Partial AUC (WPAUC), which constrains the false positive rate (FPR) to a window [$α,α+d$] to more directly align with Top-$K$ metrics. We further propose an efficient Threshold-Adjusted Windowed reweighting (TAWin) RL method for its optimization, enabling explicit control over the targeted Top-$K$ performance. Experiments on four real-world datasets validate the theory and deliver consistent state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [98] [Can QPP Choose the Right Query Variant? Evaluating Query Variant Selection for RAG Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22661)
*Negar Arabzadeh,Andrew Drozdov,Michael Bendersky,Matei Zaharia*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 探索查询性能预测(QPP)作为LLM查询改写中的变体选择机制，发现在检索和生成目标之间存在"效用差距"，且轻量级预检索预测器能以低延迟匹配或超越后检索方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的多个查询改写都需要执行完整检索-生成流程，计算开销巨大。需要一种机制在投入下游计算成本之前，选择最优的查询变体。

Method: 将QPP从传统的跨主题查询难度估计迁移到主题内判别场景，在TREC-RAG数据集上使用稀疏和密集检索器进行大规模实验，评估预检索和后检索预测器，并采用基于相关性和基于决策的度量指标。

Result: 检索指标(nDCG)和生成质量之间存在系统性分歧，即"效用差距"；QPP能够可靠地识别出能提升端到端质量的查询变体；轻量级预检索预测器在性能上通常匹配或超越更昂贵的后检索方法。

Conclusion: QPP可作为查询改写中高效变体选择的可行机制，预检索预测器在延迟-性能权衡上具有优势，为构建鲁棒且高效的RAG系统提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have made query reformulation ubiquitous in modern retrieval and Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) pipelines, enabling the generation of multiple semantically equivalent query variants. However, executing the full pipeline for every reformulation is computationally expensive, motivating selective execution: can we identify the best query variant before incurring downstream retrieval and generation costs? We investigate Query Performance Prediction (QPP) as a mechanism for variant selection across ad-hoc retrieval and end-to-end RAG. Unlike traditional QPP, which estimates query difficulty across topics, we study intra-topic discrimination - selecting the optimal reformulation among competing variants of the same information need. Through large-scale experiments on TREC-RAG using both sparse and dense retrievers, we evaluate pre- and post-retrieval predictors under correlation- and decision-based metrics. Our results reveal a systematic divergence between retrieval and generation objectives: variants that maximize ranking metrics such as nDCG often fail to produce the best generated answers, exposing a "utility gap" between retrieval relevance and generation fidelity. Nevertheless, QPP can reliably identify variants that improve end-to-end quality over the original query. Notably, lightweight pre-retrieval predictors frequently match or outperform more expensive post-retrieval methods, offering a latency-efficient approach to robust RAG.

</details>


### [99] [Aligning Dense Retrievers with LLM Utility via DistillationAligning Dense Retrievers with LLM Utility via Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22722)
*Rajinder Sandhu,Di Mu,Cheng Chang,Md Shahriar Tasjid,Himanshu Rai,Maksims Volkovs,Ga Wu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 Utility-Aligned Embeddings (UAE) 框架，通过 Utility-Modulated InfoNCE 目标训练双编码器模拟困惑度降低产生的效用分布，在 QASPER 基准上 Recall@1 提升 30.59%，MAP 提升 30.16%，Token F1 提升 17.3%，且比高效 LLM 重排序方法快 180 倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集向量检索在 RAG 中存在精度限制，而基于 LLM 重排序的效用方法虽性能优越但计算代价高且易受困惑度估计噪声影响。作者旨在融合二者优势，实现实用且高性能的检索方法。

Method: 将检索建模为分布匹配问题，使用 Utility-Modulated InfoNCE 目标训练双编码器，使其模仿由困惑度降低导出的效用分布，将分级效用信号直接注入嵌入空间，无需测试时 LLM 推理。

Result: 在 QASPER 基准上，Recall@1 提升 30.59%，MAP 提升 30.16%，Token F1 提升 17.3%，且比高效 LLM 重排序方法快 180 倍以上。

Conclusion: 将检索与生成效用对齐能够在大规模场景下提供可靠上下文，证明了该方法的实用性和高效性。

Abstract: Dense vector retrieval is the practical backbone of Retrieval- Augmented Generation (RAG), but similarity search can suffer from precision limitations. Conversely, utility-based approaches leveraging LLM re-ranking often achieve superior performance but are computationally prohibitive and prone to noise inherent in perplexity estimation. We propose Utility-Aligned Embeddings (UAE), a framework designed to merge these advantages into a practical, high-performance retrieval method. We formulate retrieval as a distribution matching problem, training a bi-encoder to imitate a utility distribution derived from perplexity reduction using a Utility-Modulated InfoNCE objective. This approach injects graded utility signals directly into the embedding space without requiring test-time LLM inference. On the QASPER benchmark, UAE improves retrieval Recall@1 by 30.59%, MAP by 30.16% and Token F1 by 17.3% over the strong semantic baseline BGE-Base. Crucially, UAE is over 180x faster than the efficient LLM re-ranking methods preserving competitive performance, demonstrating that aligning retrieval with generative utility yields reliable contexts at scale.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [100] [Focus Session: Hardware and Software Techniques for Accelerating Multimodal Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21952)
*Muhammad Shafique,Abdul Basit,Muhammad Abdullah Hanif,Alberto Marchisio,Rachmad Vidya Wicaksana Putra,Minghao Shao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多层方法论，通过硬件-软件协同设计、模型压缩（混合精度量化、结构剪枝）、推测解码、模型级联、序列长度与分辨率协同优化、数据流优化及专用硬件加速器等多种技术，高效加速多模态基础模型，并在医疗和代码生成任务上验证了有效性，同时扩展到节能型脉冲多模态模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态基础模型计算和内存需求巨大，现有加速方法缺乏系统性的多层协同优化方案。

Method: 结合硬件-软件协同设计的Transformer模块、层次感知混合精度量化与结构剪枝的MFM压缩、推测解码、小到大模型级联路由与轻量自测试、序列长度/视觉分辨率/步幅协同优化、图级算子融合、基于硬件架构的数据流优化、内存高效注意力机制、专用硬件加速器（专家设计或LLM辅助设计）。

Result: 在医疗多模态模型和代码生成任务上验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法论可有效加速多模态基础模型，并可扩展至节能型脉冲多模态模型。

Abstract: This work presents a multi-layered methodology for efficiently accelerating multimodal foundation models (MFMs). It combines hardware and software co-design of transformer blocks with an optimization pipeline that reduces computational and memory requirements. During model development, it employs performance enhancements through fine-tuning for domain-specific adaptation. Our methodology further incorporates hardware and software techniques for optimizing MFMs. Specifically, it employs MFM compression using hierarchy-aware mixed-precision quantization and structural pruning for transformer blocks and MLP channels. It also optimizes operations through speculative decoding, model cascading that routes queries through a small-to-large cascade and uses lightweight self-tests to determine when to escalate to larger models, as well as co-optimization of sequence length, visual resolution & stride, and graph-level operator fusion. To efficiently execute the model, the processing dataflow is optimized based on the underlying hardware architecture together with memory-efficient attention to meet on-chip bandwidth and latency budgets. To support this, a specialized hardware accelerator for the transformer workloads is employed, which can be developed through expert design or an LLM-aided design approach. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed methodology on medical-MFMs and on code generation tasks, and conclude with extensions toward energy-efficient spiking-MFMs.

</details>


### [101] [Performance Anomaly Detection in Athletics: A Benchmarking System with Visual Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21953)
*Blessed Madukoma,Prasenjit Mitra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套基于比赛成绩数据分析的反兴奋剂辅助系统，利用八种检测方法（从统计规则到机器学习与轨迹分析）处理2010-2025年间160万条田径成绩数据，并与官方确认的兴奋剂违规记录进行验证。轨迹类方法在检测违规与限制误报之间取得最佳平衡，系统以交互式界面支持专家研判，旨在辅助而非替代现有反兴奋剂流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生物检测成本高昂（每样本$800+），且许多禁用物质的检测窗口短，导致大量运动员无法接受常规检测。因此需要互补性的筛查方法，通过分析常规比赛成绩来识别可疑表现模式，以更高效地分配有限的反兴奋剂资源。

Method: 系统处理2010-2025年间来自19,000+场比赛的160万条田径成绩数据，采用八种检测方法，涵盖统计规则、机器学习与轨迹分析。轨迹类方法通过比较实际成绩与预期职业生涯进程来识别异常。所有方法均使用公开确认的反兴奋剂违规记录进行验证，评估其识别受制裁运动员的有效性。

Result: 轨迹类方法在检测违规与限制误报之间取得了最佳平衡。然而，所有方法均面临数据不完整和已确认违规案例稀少带来的挑战。

Conclusion: 该系统以交互式界面支持专家驱动调查，强调透明性和人类判断，旨在辅助而非替代现有的反兴奋剂流程。

Abstract: Anti-doping programs rely on biological testing to detect performance-enhancing drugs, but such testing costs over $800 per sample and is limited by short detection windows for many prohibited substances. These constraints leave large portions of athletes without regular testing, motivating complementary screening approaches that analyze routine competition results to identify suspicious performance patterns. We present a system that processes 1.6 million athletics performances from over 19,000 competitions (2010-2025) using eight detection methods ranging from statistical rules to machine learning and trajectory analysis. We validate all methods against publicly confirmed anti-doping violations to measure their effectiveness in identifying sanctioned athletes. Trajectory-based methods, which compare performances to expected career progression, achieve the best balance between detecting violations and limiting false alarms, though all methods face challenges from incomplete data and rare confirmed violations. The system provides an interactive interface for expert-driven investigation, emphasizing transparency and human judgment to support, rather than replace, established anti-doping processes.

</details>


### [102] [Conditional anomaly detection using soft harmonic functions: An application to clinical alerting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21956)
*Michal Valko,Hamed Valizadegan,Branislav Kveton,Gregory F. Cooper,Milos Hauskrecht*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于软调和解的非参数条件异常检测方法，用于识别临床数据中标签异常（如重要实验室检查遗漏）的实例，并在真实电子健康记录数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床实践中及时检测异常事件（如重要检验遗漏）十分重要，传统异常检测方法未能充分利用标签信息进行条件异常检测。

Method: 基于软调和解（soft harmonic solution）的非参数方法，估计标签置信度以检测异常错误标记，并通过正则化避免检测孤立样本及分布支持边界上的样本。

Result: 在真实电子健康记录数据集上，该方法在检测异常标签方面优于多个基线方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的非参数条件异常检测方法能有效识别临床数据中的异常标签，具有实用价值。

Abstract: Timely detection of concerning events is an important problem in clinical practice. In this paper, we consider the problem of conditional anomaly detection that aims to identify data instances with an unusual response, such as the omission of an important lab test. We develop a new non-parametric approach for conditional anomaly detection based on the soft harmonic solution, with which we estimate the confidence of the label to detect anomalous mislabeling. We further regularize the solution to avoid the detection of isolated examples and examples on the boundary of the distribution support. We demonstrate the efficacy of the proposed method in detecting unusual labels on a real-world electronic health record dataset and compare it to several baseline approaches.

</details>


### [103] [Analyzing Shapley Additive Explanations to Understand Anomaly Detection Algorithm Behaviors and Their Complementarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.00208)
*Jordan Levy,Paul Saves,Moncef Garouani,Nicolas Verstaevel,Benoit Gaudou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用SHAP解释来刻画异常检测器的决策机制，通过解释相似度衡量检测器之间的互补性，从而构建更有效的集成模型。结果表明，解释多样性与模型质量相结合能够提升无监督异常检测的集成效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督异常检测中，集成方法常因检测器依赖相似决策线索而产生冗余异常分数，难以实现真正的互补集成。现有方法缺乏对检测器决策机制差异性的量化手段。

Method: 使用SHAP值量化每个检测器对输入特征的重要性归因，通过解释轮廓（attribution profiles）衡量检测器之间的相似性，并基于解释多样性构建集成。

Result: 解释相似的检测器产生相关异常分数并识别重叠异常；解释差异可靠的指示互补检测行为。基于解释多样性的指标提供了不同于原始输出的模型选择标准。但多样性本身不足，高个体性能仍是有效集成的前提。

Conclusion: 通过显式追求解释多样性与保持模型质量相结合，可以构建更互补、更有效的无监督异常检测集成模型。

Abstract: Unsupervised anomaly detection is a challenging problem due to the diversity of data distributions and the lack of labels. Ensemble methods are often adopted to mitigate these challenges by combining multiple detectors, which can reduce individual biases and increase robustness. Yet building an ensemble that is genuinely complementary remains challenging, since many detectors rely on similar decision cues and end up producing redundant anomaly scores. As a result, the potential of ensemble learning is often limited by the difficulty of identifying models that truly capture different types of irregularities. To address this, we propose a methodology for characterizing anomaly detectors through their decision mechanisms. Using SHapley Additive exPlanations, we quantify how each model attributes importance to input features, and we use these attribution profiles to measure similarity between detectors. We show that detectors with similar explanations tend to produce correlated anomaly scores and identify largely overlapping anomalies. Conversely, explanation divergence reliably indicates complementary detection behavior. Our results demonstrate that explanation-driven metrics offer a different criterion than raw outputs for selecting models in an ensemble. However, we also demonstrate that diversity alone is insufficient; high individual model performance remains a prerequisite for effective ensembles. By explicitly targeting explanation diversity while maintaining model quality, we are able to construct ensembles that are more diverse, more complementary, and ultimately more effective for unsupervised anomaly detection.

</details>


### [104] [Multi-Task Optimization over Networks of Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21991)
*Julian Hatzky,Thomas Bartz-Beielstein,A. E. Eiben,Anil Yaman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MONET算法，将任务空间建模为图结构，通过社会学习（交叉）和个体学习（变异）在任务间进行知识迁移，在四个高维领域（最多5000个任务）中达到或超越现有MAP-Elites基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多任务优化算法存在明显局限：基于种群的方法扩展性差，难以处理大规模任务集；而能扩展到上千任务的MAP-Elites变体依赖于固定的离散化存档，忽略了任务空间的拓扑结构。

Method: 将任务空间建模为图，节点代表任务，边连接任务参数空间中邻近的任务；结合社会学习（通过相邻节点的交叉生成候选解）与个体学习（通过变异独立优化节点自身的解）。

Result: 在射箭、机械臂、倒立摆（各5000个任务）和六足机器人（2000个任务）四个领域上，MONET在所有领域均匹配或超越了现有MAP-Elites基线的性能。

Conclusion: 基于图结构的任务空间建模能够有效利用拓扑信息实现知识迁移，同时保持对高维问题的可扩展性，为大规模多任务优化提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: Multi-task optimization is a powerful approach for solving a large number of tasks in parallel. However, existing algorithms face distinct limitations: Population-based methods scale poorly and remain underexplored for large task sets. Approaches that do scale beyond a thousand tasks are mostly MAP-Elites variants and rely on a fixed, discretized archive that disregards the topology of the task space. We introduce MONET (Multi-Task Optimization over Networks of Tasks), a multi-task optimization algorithm that models the task space as a graph: tasks are nodes, and edges connect tasks in the task parameter space. This representation enables knowledge transfer between tasks and remains tractable for high-dimensional problems while exploiting the topology of the task space. MONET combines social learning, which generates candidates from neighboring nodes via crossover, with individual learning, which refines a node's own solution independently via mutation. We evaluate MONET on four domains (archery, arm, and cartpole with 5,000 tasks each; hexapod with 2,000 tasks) and show that it matches or exceeds the performance of existing MAP-Elites-based baselines across all four domains.

</details>


### [105] [ReCast: Recasting Learning Signals for Reinforcement Learning in Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22169)
*Peiyan Zhang,Hanmo Liu,Chengxuan Tong,Yuxia Wu,Wei Guo,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ReCast框架，通过修复-对比学习信号构建方法，解决了稀疏命中生成式推荐中强化学习信号不可学习的问题，在多个任务上显著提升Pass@1（最高36.6%），并以4.1%的预算达到基线性能，同时降低16.60x更新时间和16.5%峰值内存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于组的强化学习假设采样得到的 rollout 组已经是可用的学习信号，但在稀疏命中的生成式推荐场景中，许多采样组根本不可学习，这一假设不成立。

Method: 提出ReCast框架，采用"修复-对比"两阶段学习信号构建：先恢复全零组的最小可学习性，再用边界聚焦对比更新（最强正样本vs最难负样本）替代全组奖励归一化。

Result: 在多个生成式推荐任务上，ReCast相对OpenOneRec-RL实现最高36.6%的Pass@1提升；仅用4.1%的rollout预算达到基线目标性能；actor端更新时间减少16.60倍，峰值内存降低16.5%，actor MFU提升14.2%。

Conclusion: 生成式推荐的决定性RL问题不仅是如何分配奖励，更是如何从稀疏的结构化监督信号中构建可学习的优化事件；ReCast通过解耦rollout搜索宽度与actor侧更新宽度，将原本浪费的rollout预算转化为更稳定的策略更新。

Abstract: Generic group-based RL assumes that sampled rollout groups are already usable learning signals. We show that this assumption breaks down in sparse-hit generative recommendation, where many sampled groups never become learnable at all. We propose ReCast, a repair-then-contrast learning-signal framework that first restores minimal learnability for all-zero groups and then replaces full-group reward normalization with a boundary-focused contrastive update on the strongest positive and the hardest negative. ReCast leaves the outer RL framework unchanged, modifies only within-group signal construction, and partially decouples rollout search width from actor-side update width. Across multiple generative recommendation tasks, ReCast consistently outperforms OpenOneRec-RL, achieving up to 36.6% relative improvement in Pass@1. Its matched-budget advantage is substantially larger: ReCast reaches the baseline's target performance with only 4.1% of the rollout budget, and this advantage widens with model scale. The same design also yields direct system-level gains, reducing actor-side update time by 16.60x, lowering peak allocated memory by 16.5%, and improving actor MFU by 14.2%. Mechanism analysis shows that ReCast mitigates the persistent all-zero / single-hit regime, restores learnability when natural positives are scarce, and converts otherwise wasted rollout budget into more stable policy updates. These results suggest that, for generative recommendation, the decisive RL problem is not only how to assign rewards, but how to construct learnable optimization events from sparse, structured supervision.

</details>


### [106] [Universal Transformers Need Memory: Depth-State Trade-offs in Adaptive Recursive Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21999)
*Grigory Sapunov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在数独极端推理任务中，记忆令牌作为单块通用Transformer（UT）计算草稿纸的作用。实验发现记忆令牌是必要的，最佳数量为8-32个，准确率约57.4%。同时发现路由器初始化陷阱导致>70%训练失败，通过将偏置反转为-3（"深度启动"）可解决。ACT比固定深度处理更稳定（56.9% vs 53.4%），且注意力头在递归深度中分化为记忆读取器、约束传播器和集成器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索记忆令牌作为计算草稿纸在通用Transformer中的必要性及最佳配置，解决初始化陷阱问题，比较ACT与固定深度处理的性能差异。

Method: 采用单块Universal Transformer + Adaptive Computation Time架构，在Sudoku-Extreme基准上测试不同记忆令牌数量、初始化方案、ACT与固定深度处理，通过路由器偏置反转解决初始化陷阱，引入λ预热减少计算步数。

Result: 记忆令牌必要；最佳数量8-32，准确率57.4%±0.7%；路由器偏置设为-3消除初始化陷阱；ACT比固定深度更稳定（56.9%±0.7% vs 53.4%±9.3%）；λ预热减少34%步数且精度相当（57.0%±1.1%）；注意力头出现功能分化。

Conclusion: 记忆令牌对UT在组合推理任务中不可或缺，ACT优于固定深度处理，路由器初始化陷阱可被"深度启动"方案规避，注意力头在递归中自然分化为不同功能角色。

Abstract: We study learned memory tokens as computational scratchpad for a single-block Universal Transformer (UT) with Adaptive Computation Time (ACT) on Sudoku-Extreme, a combinatorial reasoning benchmark. We find that memory tokens are empirically necessary: across all configurations tested -- 3 seeds, multiple token counts, two initialization schemes, ACT and fixed-depth processing -- no configuration without memory tokens achieves non-trivial performance. The optimal count exhibits a sharp lower threshold (T=0 always fails, T=4 is borderline, T=8 reliably succeeds for 81-cell puzzles) followed by a stable plateau (T=8-32, 57.4% +/- 0.7% exact-match) and collapse from attention dilution at T=64.
  During experimentation, we identify a router initialization trap that causes >70% of training runs to fail: both default zero-bias initialization (p ~ 0.5) and Graves' recommended positive bias (p ~ 0.73) cause tokens to halt after ~2 steps at initialization, settling into a shallow equilibrium (halt ~ 5-7) that the model cannot escape. Inverting the bias to -3 ("deep start," p ~ 0.05) eliminates this failure mode. We confirm through ablation that the trap is inherent to ACT initialization, not an artifact of our architecture choices.
  With reliable training established, we show that (1) ACT provides more consistent results than fixed-depth processing (56.9% +/- 0.7% vs 53.4% +/- 9.3% across 3 seeds); (2) ACT with lambda warmup achieves matching accuracy (57.0% +/- 1.1%) using 34% fewer ponder steps; and (3) attention heads specialize into memory readers, constraint propagators, and integrators across recursive depth. Code is available at https://github.com/che-shr-cat/utm-jax.

</details>


### [107] [Mochi: Aligning Pre-training and Inference for Efficient Graph Foundation Models via Meta-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22031)
*João Mattos,Arlei Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Mochi，一种基于元学习训练框架的图基础模型，通过少样本episode预训练对齐训练与推理目标，在25个真实图数据集上取得竞争性能，同时训练时间比最强基线减少8~27倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图基础模型采用基于重建的预训练目标（如链接预测），并假设通过单独的统一化步骤（如类原型）即可将表示与下游任务对齐。本文通过实验证明该流程存在局限，直接影响下游任务性能。

Method: 采用元学习训练框架，在少样本episode上进行预训练，使训练目标与推理协议直接对齐，无需依赖后置的统一化步骤。提出Mochi和更强大的Mochi++两个变体。

Result: 在25个真实图数据集（涵盖节点分类、链接预测、图分类）上，Mochi/Mochi++达到与现有图基础模型相当或更优的性能，且训练时间仅为最强基线的1/8~1/27。

Conclusion: 基于元学习的少样本episode预训练策略能有效解决图基础模型的任务统一化和训练效率问题，优于传统的重建式预训练加后置统一化的范式。

Abstract: We propose Mochi, a Graph Foundation Model that addresses task unification and training efficiency by adopting a meta-learning based training framework. Prior models pre-train with reconstruction-based objectives such as link prediction, and assume that the resulting representations can be aligned with downstream tasks through a separate unification step such as class prototypes. We demonstrate through synthetic and real-world experiments that this procedure, while simple and intuitive, has limitations that directly affect downstream task performance. To address these limitations, Mochi pre-trains on few-shot episodes that mirror the downstream evaluation protocol, aligning the training objective with inference rather than relying on a post-hoc unification step. We show that Mochi, along with its more powerful variant Mochi++, achieves competitive or superior performance compared to existing Graph Foundation Models across 25 real-world graph datasets spanning node classification, link prediction, and graph classification, while requiring 8$\sim$27 times less training time than the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [108] [Kernel Contracts: A Specification Language for ML Kernel Correctness Across Heterogeneous Silicon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22032)
*Cooper Veit*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于描述机器学习内核合约的形式化规约语言，包含八个组成部分，覆盖十二类合约类别，并通过三个历史 incidents 验证了其有效性，旨在为内核行为一致性提供规范性参考标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前ML内核在不同硬件平台（如AMD、NVIDIA）上存在行为不一致问题（如精度差异、静默降精度、越界访问等），但缺乏形式化合约来仲裁这些争议。已有实证工作衡量了跨平台的差距，但未明确被违反的合约内容。

Method: 提出一种内核合约规约语言，包含八个部分：标识符、范围、前置条件、后置条件、容差、参考Oracle、测量协议和违规签名。定义了十二类合约类别，覆盖精度、顺序、编译器诱发的和异常值等失效模式。要求三类校准：至少一个符合参考的实现和至少一个违反合约但通过基本功能测试的实现。

Result: 将框架应用于三个历史事件：华为Ascend静默精度强制转换、Sakana AI CUDA Engineer奖励黑客、AMD越界静默接受，展示了每个非正式诊断如何映射到具体的合约违规并具有可测量的签名。

Conclusion: 内核合约套件可作为规范性参考，用于评定内核行为的一致性，类似ISASecure对工业控制系统IEC 62443的合规评级方式。

Abstract: Every ML kernel ships with an implicit contract about what it computes. People rarely write the contract down. When two kernels disagree -- when a matmul on AMD produces a different gradient than the same matmul on NVIDIA, when a fused attention kernel silently downcasts an accumulator, when an out-of-bounds access returns zero on one stack and garbage on another -- there is no formal artifact to arbitrate the dispute. Recent empirical work has measured the gap across silicon platforms, but none of it specifies the contract being violated.
  We present a specification language for kernel contracts. A contract has eight parts: identifier, scope, precondition, postcondition, tolerance, reference oracle, measurement protocol, and violation signature. We use it to state twelve contract classes covering precision, ordering, compiler-induced, and exceptional-value failure modes, each grounded in published empirical evidence. We require a three-state calibration: every contract must admit at least one reference-conforming implementation and at least one contract-violating implementation that passes basic functional tests. We apply the framework to three documented incidents -- Huawei Ascend silent precision coercion, Sakana AI CUDA Engineer reward hacking, AMD out-of-bounds silent acceptance -- and show that each informal diagnosis maps to a specific contract violation with a measurable signature. A kernel contract suite is a normative reference against which conformance can be graded, in the way that ISASecure grades industrial control systems against IEC 62443.

</details>


### [109] [LTBs-KAN: Linear-Time B-splines Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22034)
*Eduardo Said Merin-Martinez,Andres Mendez-Vazquez,Eduardo Rodriguez-Tello*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于基样条的线性时间B样条KAN（LTBs-KAN），通过线性复杂度计算和乘积和矩阵分解，显著降低了KAN的计算负担和参数量，在多个数据集上验证了其时间效率和参数压缩能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KAN虽在可解释性和表达能力上优于MLP，但因B样条函数的递归计算导致速度远慢于MLP，限制了实际应用。本文旨在解决KAN的计算效率瓶颈。

Method: 提出基样条线性时间B样条KAN（LTBs-KAN），核心包含两点：1）采用新型基样条方法替代传统的Boor-Mansfield-Cox递归算法，实现线性时间复杂度的B样条计算；2）在正向传播中引入乘积和矩阵分解，进一步减少模型参数量而不损失性能。

Result: 在MNIST、Fashion-MNIST和CIFAR-10数据集上，LTBs-KAN作为构建模块相比其他KAN实现，在时间复杂度和参数压缩方面均取得了良好效果。

Conclusion: LTBs-KAN有效解决了KAN计算速度慢和参数冗余的问题，在保持性能的同时实现了线性时间复杂度和参数压缩，为KAN的实际部署和广泛应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) are a recent neural network architecture offering an alternative to Multilayer Perceptrons (MLPs) with improved explainability and expressibility. However, KANs are significantly slower than MLPs due to the recursive nature of B-spline function computations, limiting their application. This work addresses these issues by proposing a novel base-spline Linear-Time B-splines Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (LTBs-KAN) with linear complexity. Unlike previous methods that rely on the Boor-Mansfield-Cox spline algorithm or other computationally intensive mathematical functions, our approach significantly reduces the computational burden. Additionally, we further reduce model's parameter through product-of-sums matrix factorization in the forward pass without sacrificing performance. Experiments on MNIST, Fashion-MNIST and CIFAR-10 demonstrate that LTBs-KAN achieves good time complexity and parameter reduction, when used as building architectural blocks, compared to other KAN implementations.

</details>


### [110] [LayerBoost: Layer-Aware Attention Reduction for Efficient LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22050)
*Mohamed Ali Souibgui,Jan Fostier,Rodrigo Abadía-Heredia,Bohdan Denysenko,Christian Marschke,Igor Peric*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LayerBoost提出了一种层感知的注意力削减方法，通过敏感性分析识别Transformer中不同层对性能的影响，对高敏感层保留标准softmax注意力、中等敏感层替换为线性滑动窗口注意力、低敏感层完全移除注意力，并使用轻量蒸馏进行性能恢复，在仅需1000万额外训练token的情况下实现高达68%的推理延迟降低和吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统线性或混合注意力方法在所有层均匀替换softmax注意力，导致显著性能下降或需要大量重新训练。本文旨在通过层感知的方式选择性修改注意力机制，在保持模型质量的同时大幅提升推理效率。

Method: 首先对预训练模型进行系统性敏感性分析，识别各层对性能的关键程度；然后根据分析结果应用三种策略：高敏感层保留标准softmax注意力、中等敏感层替换为线性滑动窗口注意力、低敏感层完全移除注意力；最后通过轻量级蒸馏（仅需1000万额外训练token）进行性能恢复。

Result: 在高并发场景下推理延迟降低高达68%，吞吐量显著提升；在多个基准测试中匹配基线模型性能，仅在少数基准上有轻微退化；显著优于最先进的注意力线性化方法。

Conclusion: LayerBoost特别适用于高并发服务和硬件受限部署场景，有效解决了推理成本和内存占用这两个关键瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Transformers are mostly relying on softmax attention, which introduces quadratic complexity with respect to sequence length and remains a major bottleneck for efficient inference. Prior work on linear or hybrid attention typically replaces softmax attention uniformly across all layers, often leading to significant performance degradation or requiring extensive retraining to recover model quality.
  This work proposes LayerBoost, a layer-aware attention reduction method that selectively modifies the attention mechanism based on the sensitivity of individual transformer layers. It first performs a systematic sensitivity analysis on a pretrained model to identify layers that are critical for maintaining performance. Guided by this analysis, three distinct strategies can be applied: retaining standard softmax attention in highly sensitive layers, replacing it with linear sliding window attention in moderately sensitive layers, and removing attention entirely in layers that exhibit low sensitivity.
  To recover performance after these architectural modifications, we introduce a lightweight distillation-based healing phase requiring only 10M additional training tokens. LayerBoost reduces inference latency and improves throughput by up to 68% at high concurrency, while maintaining competitive model quality. It matches base model performance on several benchmarks, exhibits only minor degradations on others, and significantly outperforms state-of-the-art attention linearization methods. These efficiency gains make our method particularly well-suited for high-concurrency serving and hardware-constrained deployment scenarios, where inference cost and memory footprint are critical bottlenecks.

</details>


### [111] [Reliability Auditing for Downstream LLM tasks in Psychiatry: LLM-Generated Hospitalization Risk Scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22063)
*Shevya Pandya,Shinjini Bose,Ananya Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对LLM在精神科风险评估中可靠性的审计方法，通过系统性评估提示设计和临床无关特征对预测住院风险的影响，发现临床无关变量会显著增加预测不稳定性和变异度，且提示变化对模型的影响具有模型依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在精神科等关键领域的临床推理可靠性尚不明确，已有研究指出算法偏见和提示敏感性，但缺乏系统性评估方法，尤其是针对精神科领域的上下文信息对模型输出的影响。
